# W2-03 code-anchored build spec

Decision `D-2026-10-17-w2-02-buildspec`. Implements `tasks/W2-03-cold-assessment.md` on the registered W2-02 replay. Use only accepted v4 public evidence, effective configuration and clock/source attestation. The six W2-02 manifest cases are the required phase-1 input; do not hand-insert accepted rows.

## Inputs, model and selection

Add `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/cold.py` with frozen `ColdEvidence` (`evidence_id`, `envelope_sha256`, `family`, `record_kind`, `role`, `asset_id`, `source_time`, `received_at`, `decided_at`, `quality`, `value`, `unit`, `event_id`, `correlation_id`, `outcome`, `source_id`, `clock_attested`), `ColdWindow` (`from_at`,`to_at`,`known_at`), `ColdExcursion`, `ColdStartChain` and `ColdAssessment`. Add pure `assess_excursion` and `assess_start_response`; IDs `cold-excursion/1`, `cold-start-response/1`. `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/adapters/postgres_assessment_store.py:query_evidence,_configuration,_persist,enqueue` is the pattern: select `accepted_evidence` joined to `ingestion_batch`, time-effective `foundation_revision` and `binding_set_revision` via `basis_from_snapshot`, and S4-05 `batch_publication_link/evidence_publication` for provenance. Require `site_id`, `source_time >= from`, `< to`, `received_at <= known_at`, and ingestion `decided_at <= known_at`. Order `(source_time,received_at,id)`. Only exact `cold-roles-1` effective row/asset/role, GOOD, calibrated primary chamber probe and attested clock is qualifying; SUSPECT, unbound, clock-skewed and contradictory records remain linked as CONTEXT/EXCLUDED with reason. Do not choose a latest publication by display name. Select the effective Foundation and binding at each evidence source time, and refuse if multiple periods overlap. `known_at` defaults to latest committed disposition watermark at request start; never a simulated clock. Basis SHA is SHA-256 of canonical JSON with keys `analytic_id,model_version,tenant_id,site_id,window,known_at,foundation_revision_ids,binding_revision_ids,source_gate,records`; records sorted by `(evidence_id,envelope_sha256)` and include both fields. `source_gate` is the exact S4-05 classification plus source IDs, not a boolean.

`assessment_worker.py:DispatchAssessmentWorker` is the orchestration pattern. Add `ColdAssessmentWorker` with `assess_explicit` and `run_once`, using `recompute_job.domain='cold_chain'`. `postgres_assessment_store.py` alone inserts `analytic_run`, `analytic_run_evidence`, `assessment_revision`, `cold_episode`, `cold_episode_revision`, `cold_episode_evidence`; `postgres_finding_store.py` alone inserts/reads `finding` and `finding_revision`. Refactor both adapters to accept one caller-owned SQLAlchemy `Connection`; worker starts one transaction, persists assessment, episode, Finding and completes the job in it. No partial projection on failure. Existing dispatch methods keep their old transactions and bytes. Duplicate basis returns the existing revision; changed basis appends a revision. Relevant cold `accepted_evidence` after intake enqueues a bracketed cold job; duplicate or rejected ordinal does not. A queued/failed newer job makes previous selected read `PENDING_RECOMPUTE` until the shared transaction commits.

## Rules and exact outcomes

Expected points are the 13 five-minute instants 19:00 through 20:00 in `[19:00,20:05)`; coverage numerator is distinct qualifying probe timestamps, denominator 13 (general windows derive their own aligned count), capped at 1. Need `>=0.8`, with no inferred path across a >10-minute gap. Above upper or below lower policy needs two consecutive GOOD samples at least five minutes apart; return needs two consecutive values inside band by at least effective `cold-return-hysteresis`. Start bounds `[last in-band,first out-of-band]`, end bounds `[last out-of-band,first stable in-band]`; initially out of band means unknown earliest start. Open episode has `duration_bounds.high=null`. Low duration is max(0, last continuously observed out-of-band point - latest start) for closed and open episodes, stopping at the first gap over ten minutes; high is latest return - earliest start only when both known. `degree_minute_bounds` is the sum of adjacent observed rectangles over intervals <=10 minutes using min/max exceedance; no gap area. `peak_observed` is the maximum observed HIGH sample or minimum observed LOW sample, never an interpolated extremum. Exposure Finding requires 15-minute low duration, >=0.8 coverage and source gate; otherwise POSSIBLE/UNKNOWN. No spoilage, part-failure or continuous curve claim. Normal -> `NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED`. Excursion -> first high at 19:15, open, duration low 45 min, high null. Backup-failed -> same exposure, door CONTEXT. Missing-probe -> 7/13, temperature UNKNOWN and no exposure Finding. Late-reading -> 12/13 at 20:05 and 13/13 at 20:10 with new basis/revision, old history retained.

Start chain groups by `(correlation_id,generator_asset_id,outage episode)` and accepted event IDs. Required steps: POWER_LOSS, START_REQUEST, START_RESULT, generator state, TRANSFER_RESULT if sensor bound, bus state, compressor state, probe response. Within 30-second attested source-clock ordering tolerance, request 19:05 -> success 19:07 -> transfer 19:08 for normal. Explicit failed result 19:05 after 19:00 request supports `OBSERVED_FAILURE` only with no conflicting GOOD success. Timeout from Foundation = 5 min; inferred failure from timeout additionally requires complete generator state coverage through timeout. Missing transfer is `UNKNOWN_STEP` for transfer only; bus state cannot replace it. Contradictory GOOD success and failure for same correlation -> `CONTRADICTORY`, never failure. Equal event-ID conflict is a rejected S2 ordinal, not an accepted contradictory outcome. Excluded clock skew/SUSPECT/unbound records never support claims. Claim levels are `OBSERVED|BOUNDED|POSSIBLE|UNKNOWN|CONTRADICTORY`.

Episode key is SHA-256 canonical JSON array `[tenant_id,site_id,kind,subject_asset_id,first_qualifying_event_or_crossing_evidence_id]` (UUIDs lowercase text), stored as `episode_key_sha256`. Stable anchor survives late evidence changing bounds. A newly supported different anchor after old invalidation creates a new episode and `split_from_episode_id`; old episode gets an immutable `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` revision and FindingRevision. Other transitions are `SUPPORT_CHANGED` when linked evidence changes and `BASIS_REFRESHED` when only effective configuration/source gate/known cutoff changes. Excursion and start response have separate Findings, can share public events, and later use one overlap group. Reuse `postgres_finding_store.py:_insert_finding,_insert_revision` with domain `cold_chain`; avoid dispatch-specific candidate-minute fields.

## Migration `0022_w2_cold_assessment.py`

After `0021`, create `cold_episode(id uuid PK,tenant_id uuid NOT NULL,site_id uuid NOT NULL,kind text CHECK IN ('EXCURSION','START_RESPONSE'),asset_id uuid NOT NULL,anchor_time timestamptz NOT NULL,episode_key_sha256 char(64) NOT NULL,split_from_episode_id uuid NULL,finding_id uuid NULL,created_at timestamptz NOT NULL)`. Unique `(tenant_id,site_id,episode_key_sha256)`; scoped composite FKs to Site, asset, optional split episode and Finding. `finding_id` stays null in this append-only slice: Finding links to episode through its immutable `claim.episode_id` and exact episode revision; no post-insert episode UPDATE fills the optional reverse pointer. Index `(site_id,kind,anchor_time)`. Create `cold_episode_revision(id uuid PK,tenant_id,site_id,episode_id uuid NOT NULL,revision_no int CHECK >0,assessment_revision_id uuid NOT NULL,basis_sha256 char(64) NOT NULL,claim_level text CHECK IN ('OBSERVED','BOUNDED','POSSIBLE','UNKNOWN','CONTRADICTORY'),lower_start,upper_start,lower_end,upper_end timestamptz NULL,duration_low,duration_high,degree_minutes_low,degree_minutes_high,peak_observed numeric NULL,coverage numeric(5,4) CHECK BETWEEN 0 AND 1,missing_steps jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]',created_at timestamptz NOT NULL)`. Unique `(episode_id,revision_no)` and `(episode_id,basis_sha256)`; scoped FKs to episode and assessment revision. Check each earliest/latest pair ordered when both present, low/high numeric pairs nonnegative and ordered when both present, JSON array shape, and peak null if no usable point. Index `(site_id,episode_id,revision_no DESC)`. Create `cold_episode_evidence(episode_revision_id uuid,tenant_id uuid,site_id uuid,evidence_id uuid,use text CHECK IN ('SUPPORT','CONTEXT','EXCLUDED'),reason text NULL,PRIMARY KEY(episode_revision_id,evidence_id,use))` with scoped FKs to episode revision and accepted evidence. Install append-only UPDATE/DELETE refusal triggers on all three; no upsert update.

Widen `finding` and `finding_revision` from `0009_finding_store.py`: `finding.subject_asset_id uuid NULL` scoped asset FK; existing `generator_asset_id` is **text**, so keep its type and only drop NOT NULL. Add domain CHECK `mini_grid` requires generator nonnull and subject null; `cold_chain` requires subject nonnull and generator null. Add unique `(tenant_id,site_id,id,domain)`; `finding_revision.domain text NOT NULL` backfill old rows `mini_grid`, remove temporary default, replace parent FK with `(tenant_id,site_id,finding_id,domain)` and preserve old mini-grid checks. For `cold_chain`, `episode_start/end`, `candidate_minutes`, `necessary_minutes`, `indeterminate_minutes` nullable; all three minute fields null. Replace bound CHECK with null-or-ordered and a separate mini-grid both-nonnull CHECK. Keep existing transition CHECK, provenance and source columns. `recompute_job.domain` is text and needs no SQL widening. Downgrade refuses if any cold episode/revision/link or cold Finding exists; otherwise restore old constraints/columns and drop only new tables/triggers/indexes. Do not delete data to force downgrade.

## Architect answers Q1-Q11 (`D-2026-10-18-w2-03-answers`)

1. **Q1, fuel trigger:** amend `0022_w2_cold_assessment.py` to drop and recreate `financial_consequence_dirty_on_finding_revision` with `AFTER INSERT ON finding_revision FOR EACH ROW WHEN (NEW.domain = 'mini_grid')`. Keep the existing `0012` function and mini-grid work behavior unchanged. Do this after adding/backfilling `finding_revision.domain` and before any cold FindingRevision can be inserted. `0022` downgrade first refuses live cold rows, drops the guarded trigger, restores the original unconditional trigger before dropping the `domain` column, and leaves the `0012` function intact. Prove a cold revision with nullable episode bounds creates zero `financial_consequence_work` rows, while a mini-grid revision still creates exactly its normal work row. W2-04 `0023` owns its separate cold consequence triggers/work; do not add cold money work to `0022`.
2. **Q2, schema:** accept the built `0022` constraints: nonnull coverage, `peak_observed IS NULL OR coverage > 0`, scoped `(tenant_id,site_id,id)` uniques needed by composite FKs, no self-split, lowercase 64-hex SHA checks. Retain the existing mini-grid nonnull bound/minute checks.
3. **Q3, identity:** store `analytic_id='cold-excursion'`, `model_version='1'` (and `cold-start-response`/`1`) in existing analytic tables. The display/model identifier `cold-excursion/1` is their joined form, not the stored analytic ID.
4. **Q4, duration:** for an open episode, `duration_low=max(0,last continuously observed out-of-band source_time - latest_start)`. A >10-minute gap stops that continuous run. The half-open window end is a selection boundary, not an observed reading. For the 19:15 to 20:00 canonical run the lower bound is **45 min**, not 50. `duration_high` and both end bounds remain null until a supported return. For a closed episode, lower duration uses the last continuously observed out-of-band point, and upper duration needs both known start and return bounds.
5. **Q5, earliest start:** null unless the preceding qualifying in-band point is no more than 10 minutes before the first out-of-band point. A preceding out-of-band or excluded point cannot supply it.
6. **Q6, levels:** coverage below `0.8` gives assessment/episode `UNKNOWN` and no exposure Finding; conflicting GOOD same-instant probe values give `CONTRADICTORY`. With adequate coverage, a confirmed excursion with lower duration at least 15 min and a permitting gate is `BOUNDED` with an exposure Finding; a shorter confirmed excursion or a closed gate is `POSSIBLE` without one. A lone out-of-band point is assessment state `UNKNOWN`, level `POSSIBLE`. Fully in-band is `NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED`/`BOUNDED` with no excursion episode or Finding. Do not turn a closed gate into a positive Finding.
7. **Q7, start Finding:** only `OBSERVED_FAILURE` plus permitting gate opens a start-response Finding. An explicit GOOD failure without conflicting GOOD success qualifies; a timeout qualifies only with complete generator-state coverage through its effective deadline. `OBSERVED_SUCCESS`, `UNKNOWN` and `CONTRADICTORY` do not open one.
8. **Q8, transfer:** only a qualifying `TRANSFER_RESULT` before the first `POWER_RESTORED` in the same correlation counts as backup transfer. Bus state can be observed while `TRANSFER` remains `MISSING`; a post-restoration grid transfer cannot close the backup step.
9. **Q9, peak:** for HIGH, `peak_observed=max(qualifying observed temperatures)`; for LOW, `min(...)`. Preserve the direction with the episode result/read. No interpolation or mock value supplies the peak.
10. **Q10, calibration:** resolve calibration while selecting the effective Foundation/`cold-roles-1` binding and primary room probe via `probe_asset_id`; pass only that eligible probe to the pure `ColdEvidence` rule. The pure record has no calibration field. Missing, duplicate or overlapping effective calibration/role periods fail closed.
11. **Q11, contradiction:** link each mutually conflicting accepted GOOD record as `cold_episode_evidence.use='EXCLUDED', reason='CONTRADICTORY'` (and show it in read exclusions). An S2 `EVENT_ID_CONFLICT` ordinal is not accepted evidence and never becomes this analytic contradiction.

## Commits 3-5 exact contract

## Known deviations / interpretations to carry into the packet
1. Work window: the spec says "bracket accepted cold source times into work windows" without a rule. Built: the job window is the 5-min-aligned bracket of its accepted source times, merged with every selected cold window within the 10-min bridge; the ledger coalesces pending cold jobs across the same bridge. Without it, the per-tick gateway batches produced thirteen 5-minute windows. The canonical replay yields exactly `[19:00,20:05)` (asserted). Worth an Architect confirmation.
2. Clock attestation: the cold lab installs none, and the rules need attested clocks, so the replay tests record the SYSTEM attester's labelled simulated attestation per lab source (epoch `cold-<case>`) before upload. This is not an accepted-row insert.
3. L3 `split_from_finding_id`: an ad hoc reading can only enter through the gateway outside the registered publication (the registered release refuses it: `STAGE_NOT_ATTESTABLE`), so the window's S4-05 gate becomes MIXED and correctly opens no new Finding (asserted). The positive link is proven with a test-only `classify` double holding the registered class (`test_split_links_finding_when_gate_permits`).
4. A Finding whose episode no longer warrants one (still supported, claim limited) gets `SUPPORT_CHANGED`/`BASIS_REFRESHED` with lifecycle `UNCONFIRMED`. The spec is silent.
5. `test_w200_source_events_postgres.py` "no dispatch job" now excludes `cold_chain` (W2-03 supersedes "v4 enqueues nothing" for cold roles only).
6. In the W2-02 excursion replay the start chain is `OBSERVED_FAILURE` (it opens a start Finding too). The spec states no excursion start result, so none is asserted.
