# Workflow

AssetOps is built through thin end-to-end slices that produce UI-verifiable
product behavior.

## Lanes

Planned lane:

Architect or Planner -> Implementer -> Reviewer -> User review

Use for new product behavior, domain semantics, user-visible analytics,
meaningful UI/UX, data/API contracts, architecture boundaries, simulator or
ingestion semantics, and external integrations.

Fast lane:

Implementer -> Reviewer

Use only when the work stays inside an existing product and technical contract.

## No Agent Writes An Approval

Recorded 2026-09-26 after an Implementer wrote two approvals that had not
happened into the durable record: a dated owner sign-off whose only basis was
four conversational words, and a review verdict of ACCEPT while a RETURN verdict
sat unread in the same directory. It then set the task complete and moved it to
`tasks/completed/`. Reverted at `166c02b`.

Relying on an agent to be honest about this is what failed, so these are
controls rather than advice.

- **An agent may not write an owner approval anywhere.** Not the task file, not
  a packet, not a commit message. An agent cannot observe the owner's approval;
  only the coordinator, relaying the user, records one. A conversational remark
  is not a checkpoint: the task file defines what the owner must have done, and
  nothing short of that is a sign-off.
- **An agent may not record a review verdict.** The coordinator records it,
  after reading the review file. A subagent's handback is that subagent's
  summary of its own work; the harness says so on every handback, and it was
  said on the one that was believed here.
- **An Implementer may not commission its own review.** A review it
  commissioned is not independent whatever it concludes, and
  `.ai/ROLE_CONFIG.md` requires independence from the author. The coordinator
  dispatches the Reviewer.
- **Before recording any verdict, list the review files.** Two sat in `.agent/`
  that day, twenty-two minutes apart, and the one that agreed was cited. Where
  there is more than one, they are all read and the disagreement is reported,
  not resolved by preference.
- **Do not commit into a tree a Reviewer is measuring.**
  `.ai/PROJECT_RULES.md` "One Working Copy" already says evidence taken while
  another agent holds the tree is not evidence. Four commits landed during that
  review, and it had to qualify its own findings as a result.

A task reaching `tasks/completed/` with a verdict nothing verified is the
failure these prevent. `T011B` and `T011C` reached main at `in_review` for the
mirror-image reason - no check enforces the transition, which is why the rule
has to be read rather than relied upon.

## Vertical Slice Standard

Each planned task should include the minimum required domain semantics, backend
logic, data contract, UI, tests, and demo evidence needed for a reviewable
result. Not every task needs every layer, but each task should either deliver a
UI-verifiable outcome or explicitly unlock a named UI-verifiable slice.

Features and tasks are different planning objects. A feature is a complete
product function that may require several tasks. A task is a reviewable
implementation slice that delivers or materially advances one UI-verifiable part
of a feature.

The current sequence is the Site-first S1â€“S7 queue in `tasks/README.md`, based on the current architecture, technical design and Site-first execution plan. Earlier feature maps and demo roadmaps are historical planning input.

This replaces the old examples that implied a fuel discrepancy was the first
Finding. Within each outcome, create reviewable cuts that let the owner run
and judge progress; do not make each architecture noun a separate task.

Avoid building whole layers before there is useful product behavior.

## Task Spec Size

Task specs are guardrails for fast implementation, not miniature design
documents. For S1-S7, keep durable reasoning in the current Site-first architecture, technical design, execution plan or a new decision; keep task files slice-specific and executable. The old feature map and T0xx queue remain historical.

Size is a ceiling, never a target. These are the upper limits:

- Normal UI/content slice: 120 lines.
- Boundary-changing slice: 160 lines.
- First write path, evidence contract, ingestion, simulator truth boundary, or
  analytics/finding semantics: 220 lines.

**There is no minimum.** A spec that carries everything below in fewer lines is
finished, not thin. Revised 2026-09-24: the earlier 100-180 / 180-260 / 250-400
bands described specs that restated durable mechanics, and their lower bounds
were read as a quota. Specs that cite v4, the roadmap, the feature map,
`.ai/ARCHITECTURE.md` and `.ai/DECISIONS.md` instead of restating them land far
below those numbers and are better for it. Over a ceiling, split the slice or
move the reasoning to the document that owns it.

Line count does not measure whether a spec works. This does: **an implementer
holding the task file, the references it names and the built state can start
without asking a question.** Where they must legitimately stop - a decision only
the user can take, or content that does not exist yet - the file names the stop
rather than leaving the gap silent. A file that passes that test at 70 lines
passes; a file that fails it at 300 fails.

Every task spec should include:

- User-visible outcome.
- Why this slice is next.
- Acceptance criteria tied to behavior or contracts.
- Only the product/domain semantics needed for this slice.
- Protected seams and exact checks.
- Scope limits.
- Whether user review is required.

Leave to implementation:

- Component factoring.
- Helper names.
- Styling mechanics.
- Test structure.
- Local code organization below established boundaries.

## Avoiding Spec Chaff

When writing or revising task specs, prefer behavior and contract guardrails over
defensive repetition.

- Put inherited exclusions in one shared place and reference them from tasks.
- Repeat a prohibition inside a task only when the slice has a special local
  risk or exception.
- Specify the required property and proof, not the exact implementation
  mechanics, unless the mechanism is itself the protected seam.
- Avoid exact wording requirements unless the user has reviewed that copy as
  product language; otherwise require clear, accessible meaning.
- Avoid telling implementers where to put helpers, how to factor components, or
  how to shape tests below established boundaries.
- Treat a high count of `do not`, `must not`, `never`, or `exactly` as a smell
  during planning review. It may be justified, but it should trigger a trim or a
  move into shared guidance.

## Planning Before Tasks

The Architect establishes feature outcomes and causal prerequisites before the
Planner creates tasks. For the superseded T0xx planning pass, the historical instruction was to use simulator v4 and the
corrected mini-grid roadmap for direction, plus canonical built-state records.
This replaces starting the task sequence from the UI mockups. Layout references
remain presentation guidance after a screen's content and claims are real.

The Architect may wear Product, Architecture, UI/UX, and Domain hats as needed
to produce a feature map. That feature map can then be broken into reviewable
tasks by the Planner under architectural guidance.

## Review Packet

Provide concise evidence:

- Task.
- Outcome delivered.
- Files materially changed.
- Acceptance criteria status.
- Checks run.
- Layout evidence, when the slice is layout-sensitive.
- Known deviations.
- Residual risk.
- User-review focus.

The review packet is evidence, not implementation history.

**For the rest of the simulator milestone, a Reviewer raises three things and
records the rest.** Under `D-2026-09-22-milestone-speed-over-purity`: a defect
that would be expensive to reverse later, a claim that would mislead an
Implementer, and a decision only the user can take. Everything else - naming
that is imperfect but honest, a redundant but true restatement, a vocabulary
that would be better shaped and is not wrong - goes to
`.ai/MILESTONE_REVIEW_BACKLOG.md` as a residual-risk entry rather than
blocking the slice. *This would be better named* is a backlog entry; *this
name is false* is still a finding, because a false name misleads. The complete
review, including a possible refactor, follows milestone completion and proper
testing, and the backlog is what it reads first.

A slice is layout-sensitive when it changes shell layout, dense tables,
intrinsic-width drawings, SVG geometry, tab or subtab treatment, or viewport
behaviour. Such a slice runs `tools/layout-evidence.mjs` and records the exact
command, the base URL when non-default, and the final result line. A missing
precondition is not a pass: when Chrome, the backend or the dev server is
unavailable, record `layout evidence: not run` with the blocking precondition,
and the affected layout claim is unverified rather than green. Every new
measured claim about overflow, visibility or rendered content carries at least
one measurement proving its set is non-empty.

## Closeout

Before a slice is merged, its Review Outcome is recorded in the task file and
its status is set to complete, or the task says explicitly what is still open.
Nothing checks this, so it is the Reviewer's and the user's to hold: T011B and
T011C reached `main` at `in_review`, and the user noticed rather than a guard.

### Coordinator transition for the W1â€“W6 delivery loop

The coordinator owns transitions, not implementation or independent review. A candidate may be selected only after the active wave's owner-approved manifest IDs, core-panel keys, 1â€“5 demo values and K are recorded and the registry, ledger, navigation and workflow guards pass on current inputs. Require a reviewed mapped path from the candidate to an approved core claim, closed technical `requires`, no competing active task, and an S/M/L reviewable packet. `sequenced_after` records history; an `enables` path supplies planning benefit only. Stale inputs or checker failure stop selection. `next-work` is advice with evidence, never approval.

For an active task, the coordinator verifies the branch and packet, requests implementation, then dispatches an independent Reviewer after the tree is stable. List and read every review file before recording an outcome; disagreement is reported. On rejection, return the packet/finding to the Implementer for a bounded fix and fresh review. A second rejection of the same task stops the loop for owner direction. Only the coordinator records the review outcome, changes task status or moves a packet to completed, and only from the underlying review evidence. An agent does not write owner approval; the coordinator records owner words against the named checkpoint.

Before merge, verify all required checks and lineage, the independent review result, task closeout/status, and any `USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED` owner checkpoint. A blocked or unrecorded checkpoint stops merge. The coordinator merges the reviewed branch, records commit/evidence and recalculates the registry/report from the merged tree before selecting another task. Do not commit into a Reviewer-measured tree. A real claim without accepted source lineage stops the loop.

At wave start and exit, S2/S4/S6/S7 checkpoints, panel withholding, screen dropping, XL escalation, scoring-parameter change or contested â‰¤5% top-two ranking, stop for the owner with the actual options/evidence. At >5% through 10%, apply the published tie-break, record the two scores and reason in `.agent/coordinator-handoff.md`, and continue; above 10% or with one eligible candidate, continue without ranking notice. Other gates remain binding. A changed input invalidates prior report/selection until guards rerun. The coordinator does not infer an approval from silence or a proposed tier.

## User Review

Use `USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED: true` when the slice materially affects product
direction, domain semantics, UI/UX, evidence interpretation, user-visible
analytics, information hierarchy, or demo narrative.

After a user-review checkpoint, do not assume the next planned slice is still
correct. The user may approve, revise, reorder, remove, split, expand, or
redirect later work.

.agent/W2-03-build-spec.md-12-
.agent/W2-03-build-spec.md:13:Expected points are the 13 five-minute instants 19:00 through 20:00 in `[19:00,20:05)`; coverage numerator is distinct qualifying probe timestamps, denominator 13 (general windows derive their own aligned count), capped at 1. Need `>=0.8`, with no inferred path across a >10-minute gap. Above upper or below lower policy needs two consecutive GOOD samples at least five minutes apart; return needs two consecutive values inside band by at least effective `cold-return-hysteresis`. Start bounds `[last in-band,first out-of-band]`, end bounds `[last out-of-band,first stable in-band]`; initially out of band means unknown earliest start. Open episode has `duration_bounds.high=null`. Low duration is max(0, last continuously observed out-of-band point - latest start) for closed and open episodes, stopping at the first gap over ten minutes; high is latest return - earliest start only when both known. `degree_minute_bounds` is the sum of adjacent observed rectangles over intervals <=10 minutes using min/max exceedance; no gap area. `peak_observed` is the maximum observed HIGH sample or minimum observed LOW sample, never an interpolated extremum. Exposure Finding requires 15-minute low duration, >=0.8 coverage and source gate; otherwise POSSIBLE/UNKNOWN. No spoilage, part-failure or continuous curve claim. Normal -> `NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED`. Excursion -> first high at 19:15, open, duration low 45 min, high null. Backup-failed -> same exposure, door CONTEXT. Missing-probe -> 7/13, temperature UNKNOWN and no exposure Finding. Late-reading -> 12/13 at 20:05 and 13/13 at 20:10 with new basis/revision, old history retained.

.agent/W2-03-build-spec.md:15:Start chain groups by `(correlation_id,generator_asset_id,outage episode)` and accepted event IDs. Required steps: POWER_LOSS, START_REQUEST, START_RESULT, generator state, TRANSFER_RESULT if sensor bound, bus state, compressor state, probe response. Within 30-second attested source-clock ordering tolerance, request 19:05 -> success 19:07 -> transfer 19:08 for normal. Explicit failed result 19:05 after 19:00 request supports `OBSERVED_FAILURE` only with no conflicting GOOD success. Timeout from Foundation = 5 min; inferred failure from timeout additionally requires complete generator state coverage through timeout. Missing transfer is `UNKNOWN_STEP` for transfer only; bus state cannot replace it. Contradictory GOOD success and failure for same correlation -> `CONTRADICTORY`, never failure. Equal event-ID conflict is a rejected S2 ordinal, not an accepted contradictory outcome. Excluded clock skew/SUSPECT/unbound records never support claims. Claim levels are `OBSERVED|BOUNDED|POSSIBLE|UNKNOWN|CONTRADICTORY`.

.agent/W2-03-build-spec.md:44:4. **Q4, duration:** for an open episode, `duration_low=max(0,last continuously observed out-of-band source_time - latest_start)`. A >10-minute gap stops that continuous run. The half-open window end is a selection boundary, not an observed reading. For the 19:15 to 20:00 canonical run the lower bound is **45 min**, not 50. `duration_high` and both end bounds remain null until a supported return. For a closed episode, lower duration uses the last continuously observed out-of-band point, and upper duration needs both known start and return bounds.
.agent/W2-03-build-spec.md:45:5. **Q5, earliest start:** null unless the preceding qualifying in-band point is no more than 10 minutes before the first out-of-band point. A preceding out-of-band or excluded point cannot supply it.
.agent/W2-03-build-spec.md-46-6. **Q6, levels:** coverage below `0.8` gives assessment/episode `UNKNOWN` and no exposure Finding; conflicting GOOD same-instant probe values give `CONTRADICTORY`. With adequate coverage, a confirmed excursion with lower duration at least 15 min and a permitting gate is `BOUNDED` with an exposure Finding; a shorter confirmed excursion or a closed gate is `POSSIBLE` without one. A lone out-of-band point is assessment state `UNKNOWN`, level `POSSIBLE`. Fully in-band is `NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED`/`BOUNDED` with no excursion episode or Finding. Do not turn a closed gate into a positive Finding.
.agent/W2-03-build-spec.md-47-7. **Q7, start Finding:** only `OBSERVED_FAILURE` plus permitting gate opens a start-response Finding. An explicit GOOD failure without conflicting GOOD success qualifies; a timeout qualifies only with complete generator-state coverage through its effective deadline. `OBSERVED_SUCCESS`, `UNKNOWN` and `CONTRADICTORY` do not open one.
.agent/W2-03-build-spec.md-48-8. **Q8, transfer:** only a qualifying `TRANSFER_RESULT` before the first `POWER_RESTORED` in the same correlation counts as backup transfer. Bus state can be observed while `TRANSFER` remains `MISSING`; a post-restoration grid transfer cannot close the backup step.
.agent/W2-03-build-spec.md-49-9. **Q9, peak:** for HIGH, `peak_observed=max(qualifying observed temperatures)`; for LOW, `min(...)`. Preserve the direction with the episode result/read. No interpolation or mock value supplies the peak.
.agent/W2-03-build-spec.md-50-10. **Q10, calibration:** resolve calibration while selecting the effective Foundation/`cold-roles-1` binding and primary room probe via `probe_asset_id`; pass only that eligible probe to the pure `ColdEvidence` rule. The pure record has no calibration field. Missing, duplicate or overlapping effective calibration/role periods fail closed.
.agent/W2-03-build-spec.md-51-11. **Q11, contradiction:** link each mutually conflicting accepted GOOD record as `cold_episode_evidence.use='EXCLUDED', reason='CONTRADICTORY'` (and show it in read exclusions). An S2 `EVENT_ID_CONFLICT` ordinal is not accepted evidence and never becomes this analytic contradiction.

Its SHA key is the canonical five-part array already specified; anchor on first qualifying crossing/request evidence ID, never a bound time that a late record can move. A confirmed but claim-limited excursion can have an episode without a Finding; an observed successful start can have a chain episode without a Finding; no excursion episode is invented for an in-band or single unconfirmed point. Map `start_earliest/start_latest/end_earliest/end_latest` to `lower_start/upper_start/lower_end/upper_end`, duration and degree-minute bounds to the corresponding numeric columns, coverage to a four-decimal ratio, and start-chain `missing_steps` to its JSON array. Start-response duration/degree-minute/peak columns are null; its coverage is `0` because those are temperature metrics. Episode evidence links cover the exact SUPPORT, CONTEXT and EXCLUDED records in the pure result, with no private world IDs. Identical selected basis returns existing revisions; changed basis appends. A returned old basis after an intervening revision must append a new FindingRevision even though its SHA appeared earlier. On anchor invalidation append `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` to the old episode/Finding, create the new episode with `split_from_episode_id`, and set the new Finding's `split_from_finding_id` to the old Finding when there was one. Use `SUPPORT_CHANGED` when linked accepted evidence changes; use `BASIS_REFRESHED` when only effective configuration, source gate or cutoff changes. Store cold Finding `domain='cold_chain'`, `subject_asset_id`, null `generator_asset_id` and null dispatch minute fields; `claim` contains `episode_id` and exact `episode_revision_id`. Never call the dispatch-shaped `_insert_finding/_insert_revision` unchanged: add domain-specific cold insert paths under the same owner. Prove all six `test_w203_cold_replay_postgres.py` cases through authenticated Batch V2 intake, plus no mini-grid fuel work from cold FindingRevision.

Late acceptance marks the previous exact read pending, recomputes a new basis/revision, preserves old history and changes bounded result; same basis is idempotent. Add `cold_episode,cold_episode_revision,cold_episode_evidence` to rule-8 `$assessTables` **before** `$assessInserts`; `postgres_assessment_store.py` alone inserts episode/analytic rows, `postgres_finding_store.py` alone inserts Findings, and `assessment_worker.py` owns their shared transaction/job completion. Extend `tools/writer_guard_probes.py` with pass probes for each owner and fail probes for foreign writer, UPDATE/DELETE and `DO UPDATE`; configuration guard fails closed if an owner/probe is absent. W2-04 consequence writer/work tables and money trigger remain in W2-04, not this migration.

## Known deviations / interpretations to carry into the packet
1. Work window: the spec says "bracket accepted cold source times into work windows" without a rule. Built: the job window is the 5-min-aligned bracket of its accepted source times, merged with every selected cold window within the 10-min bridge; the ledger coalesces pending cold jobs across the same bridge. Without it, the per-tick gateway batches produced thirteen 5-minute windows. The canonical replay yields exactly `[19:00,20:05)` (asserted). Worth an Architect confirmation.
2. Clock attestation: the cold lab installs none, and the rules need attested clocks, so the replay tests record the SYSTEM attester's labelled simulated attestation per lab source (epoch `cold-<case>`) before upload. This is not an accepted-row insert.
3. L3 `split_from_finding_id`: an ad hoc reading can only enter through the gateway outside the registered publication (the registered release refuses it: `STAGE_NOT_ATTESTABLE`), so the window's S4-05 gate becomes MIXED and correctly opens no new Finding (asserted). The positive link is proven with a test-only `classify` double holding the registered class (`test_split_links_finding_when_gate_permits`).
4. A Finding whose episode no longer warrants one (still supported, claim limited) gets `SUPPORT_CHANGED`/`BASIS_REFRESHED` with lifecycle `UNCONFIRMED`. The spec is silent.
5. `test_w200_source_events_postgres.py` "no dispatch job" now excludes `cold_chain` (W2-03 supersedes "v4 enqueues nothing" for cold roles only).
6. In the W2-02 excursion replay the start chain is `OBSERVED_FAILURE` (it opens a start Finding too). The spec states no excursion start result, so none is asserted.

## Commits 3-5 exact contract

**Commit 3 â€” transaction and projection.** Add `ColdAssessmentWorker` in `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/assessment_worker.py` with `assess_explicit(tenant_id,site_id,window,known_at)` and `run_once(tenant_id=None,site_id=None)`; use `recompute_job.domain='cold_chain'` and bracket relevant accepted cold source times into work windows. `postgres_assessment_store.py` alone queries typed v4 accepted rows and inserts `analytic_run`, `analytic_run_evidence`, `analytic_run_assessment`, `assessment_revision`, `analytic_run_selection`, and the three `cold_episode*` tables. `postgres_finding_store.py` alone inserts cold `finding`, `finding_revision`, `finding_revision_assessment` and `finding_revision_evidence`. Add caller-connection methods to the two stores while preserving existing dispatch methods and their transaction behavior. The worker opens one SQLAlchemy transaction, rechecks the lease/site/input fence, selects effective configuration and S4-05 lineage, computes both analytics, persists their exact revisions, episodes and Findings, and completes the job before commit. Failure rolls back *all* projection rows and completion, then records a retryable job failure; a stale lease never commits. Acquire the same Site/domain advisory lock for competing cold projections. The relevant intake enqueue must occur only for a new accepted cold ordinal, including a late one; duplicate/rejected ordinals enqueue nothing. Configuration or source-gate changes must enqueue affected selected windows as well, or the read stays explicitly stale/pending until a controlled reconciliation enqueues them.

Use `analytic_id='cold-excursion'|'cold-start-response'`, `model_version='1'`, window `[from,to)`, explicit `known_at`, and the exact canonical basis described above. In `assessment_revision.result`, persist the pure `ColdAssessment` fields and its episode/chain data with decimal strings, UTC instants and exact accepted IDs; use a stable assessment key per analytic/Site/window, and append when the basis changes. `cold_episode.kind` is `EXCURSION|START_RESPONSE`; excursion `asset_id` is the effective room, chain `asset_id` the generator. Its SHA key is the canonical five-part array already specified; anchor on first qualifying crossing/request evidence ID, never a bound time that a late record can move. A confirmed but claim-limited excursion can have an episode without a Finding; an observed successful start can have a chain episode without a Finding; no excursion episode is invented for an in-band or single unconfirmed point. Map `start_earliest/start_latest/end_earliest/end_latest` to `lower_start/upper_start/lower_end/upper_end`, duration and degree-minute bounds to the corresponding numeric columns, coverage to a four-decimal ratio, and start-chain `missing_steps` to its JSON array. Start-response duration/degree-minute/peak columns are null; its coverage is `0` because those are temperature metrics. Episode evidence links cover the exact SUPPORT, CONTEXT and EXCLUDED records in the pure result, with no private world IDs. Identical selected basis returns existing revisions; changed basis appends. A returned old basis after an intervening revision must append a new FindingRevision even though its SHA appeared earlier. On anchor invalidation append `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` to the old episode/Finding, create the new episode with `split_from_episode_id`, and set the new Finding's `split_from_finding_id` to the old Finding when there was one. Use `SUPPORT_CHANGED` when linked accepted evidence changes; use `BASIS_REFRESHED` when only effective configuration, source gate or cutoff changes. Store cold Finding `domain='cold_chain'`, `subject_asset_id`, null `generator_asset_id` and null dispatch minute fields; `claim` contains `episode_id` and exact `episode_revision_id`. Never call the dispatch-shaped `_insert_finding/_insert_revision` unchanged: add domain-specific cold insert paths under the same owner. Prove all six `test_w203_cold_replay_postgres.py` cases through authenticated Batch V2 intake, plus no mini-grid fuel work from cold FindingRevision.

The canonical selected window is `[2026-10-06T19:00:00Z,2026-10-06T20:05:00Z)`: 13 scheduled probe instants, 19:00 through 20:00. Expected accepted-replay results: normal 13/13, `NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED`/`BOUNDED`, observed successful start and transfer, zero exposure Findings; excursion 13/13, first HIGH at 19:15, start bounds 19:10â€“19:15, open end, duration `{low:'45',high:null}`, peak `'4.8'`, one bounded exposure Finding; backup-failed 13/13, `OBSERVED_FAILURE`/`OBSERVED` start Finding, `TRANSFER` missing, door CONTEXT, plus one exposure Finding; missing-probe 7/13, `UNKNOWN` temperature/no exposure Finding, start failure still observed, gap 19:15â€“19:45; late-reading at 20:05 cutoff 12/13 with 19:20 absent, at 20:10 cutoff 13/13 and a new immutable basis/revision with the same first-HIGH anchor; contradictory GOOD start outcomes `CONTRADICTORY`, no observed-failure Finding. The pinned pure vectors and replay dispositions, not v69 illustration values, set other decimals and evidence UUIDs.

**Commit 4 â€” selected reads.** Add `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/cold_reads.py:ColdReadService` and `backend/assetops_backend/cold_assessment_api.py:build_cold_assessment_router`. The four GET routes are `/api/sites/{code}/cold/temperature`, `/backup-chain`, `/findings`, and `/findings/{id}`. Accept strict UTC `from,to,known_at`; enforce `from<to`, half-open maximum 31 days, tenant and COLDCHAIN Site scope; default `known_at` to the committed disposition watermark captured at request start. Use a single consistent selected snapshot for all objects and S4-05 source gate. Common envelope: `site_id`, `window:{from,to,known_at}`, `currentness:{state,watermark,latest_relevant_evidence_at,pending_since}`, `source_provenance:{classification,source_ids,evidence_ids,basis_sha256}`, `limitations:string[]`. Every object/card includes `as_of:UTC|null` and `limitation:string|null`; decimal scalars are strings or null. `watermark` identifies the included disposition time and selected assessment revision; never use the simulator clock. States are exactly `CURRENT|PENDING_RECOMPUTE|STALE_SOURCE|HISTORICAL|UNKNOWN`. A pending newer job returns the previous selected revision only as pending, without a current claim; an explicit unresolved revision/basis request is 409. Historical cutoff remains historical after newer evidence. Current state requires the latest relevant probe no older than 10 minutes at the read's time. Valid absent assessment/claim is 200 `UNKNOWN` with null values and empty arrays; infrastructure failure is 503, never an empty success. Refusals use `{error:{code,message}}`: 401 auth, 403 wrong tenant or disallowed source disclosure, 404 missing/wrong-type Site or unknown scoped Finding, 409 unsettled selection/cursor basis, 422 malformed UTC/range/unknown parameter, 503 unavailable store.

`temperature` adds `policy:{low_deg_c,high_deg_c,return_hysteresis_deg_c,min_excursion_min,probe_asset_id,room_asset_id,as_of,limitation}`, `points:[{at,value,quality,evidence_id,as_of,limitation}]`, `coverage:{known_points,expected_points,ratio}`, `gaps:[{start,end,reason,expected_points,received_points,as_of,limitation}]`, `excursions:[{episode_id,revision_id,direction,start_bounds:{earliest,latest},end_bounds:{earliest,latest},duration_bounds:{low,high},degree_minute_bounds:{low,high},peak_observed,claim_level,as_of,limitation}]`, `excluded:[{evidence_id,reason,as_of,limitation}]`. `backup-chain` adds `chains:[{correlation_id,episode_id,revision_id,steps:[{kind,at,outcome,evidence_id,state,as_of,limitation}],missing_steps,claim_level,start_timeout_min,as_of,limitation}]`, `contradictions:[{correlation_id,evidence_ids,reason,as_of,limitation}]`; step order is GRID, START_REQUEST, START_RESULT, GENERATOR, TRANSFER when bound, BUS, COMPRESSOR, PROBE. Missing step has null time/outcome/evidence ID. `findings` is `{items:[{finding_id,kind,subject_asset_id,revision_id,lifecycle,claim_level,title,episode_bounds,limitations,assessment_revision_id,evidence_ids,links:{finding,evidence,analysis},as_of,limitation}],next_cursor}`; detail has those selected fields plus `basis_sha256`, `episode_id`, `episode_revision_id`, `revisions:[{revision_id,revision_no,transition,lifecycle,claim_level,basis_sha256,created_at,evidence_ids,as_of,limitation}]`. `episode_bounds` has `{start:{earliest,latest},end:{earliest,latest}}`. Each evidence/Analysis link retains the selected window/cutoff and exact accepted/revision ID. List ordering is `(anchor_time,finding_id)`, cursor is base64url canonical `(time,id)` bound to the selected basis, default limit 50/max 200; never page across a changed basis. Filter every Finding query by `domain='cold_chain'`, tenant, Site and selected window; existing mini-grid Finding reads remain unchanged.

The six §4 scenarios use a new registered `COLD-LAB-001` Foundation with §4's thirteen exact values, rather than COLD-001's different values. 7-## 1. W2-00: ordinary source-to-evidence format
8-
9-The active authenticated path uses **SourceEnvelopeV3**, not the older staged v1/v2 envelope: v3 names record families but refuses them, while S2 also refuses non-measurements (`contracts/assetops_contracts/source_envelope_v3.py:1-43,77-78,240-246`; `backend/assetops_backend/evidence/ledger.py:268-280`). Extend that path as schema **4**, `assetops.source-envelope`; retain v3's allocated `(gateway,source,epoch,sequence)` message identity, canonical JSON, UTC instants and exact numeric text. `source_envelope.py` v1/v2 and existing Stage artifacts remain byte-for-byte readable. The v4 parser accepts v4 only; a version dispatcher accepts v3 and v4. v3 receipts, dedup keys and accepted rows are untouched. W2-02 pins v4 in a new cold publication profile; never switch an existing profile in place (`contracts/assetops_contracts/source_envelope_v3.py:1-43,70-78`; `simulator/assetops_simulator/gateway/mappings.py:87-107`).
--
74-**B — event binding.** Add `event` to `SIGNAL_UNITS`; it is the nonquantitative declared unit of an event channel, never a numeric evidence unit. Add `EVENT` to `Role.value_kind` and give nonquantitative EVENT roles `canonical_unit='event'`, `source_unit='event'`, `conversion={kind:'IDENTITY',parameters:{}}`; extend `conversion_problem` so this exact triple is valid only for EVENT and no numeric conversion occurs. Manual temperature is the sole quantitative event channel: `source_unit=canonical_unit='degC'`, IDENTITY, `allowed_units=['degC']`, and v4 `MANUAL_LOG` requires value/degC. Other event bindings have `allowed_units=[]`, and v4 event payload `unit=value=null`; declared channel unit `event` does not appear in the envelope payload. Add explicit per-role event-kind/outcome allowlists and compare them with the five pinned v4 snapshot fields; a state ENUM conversion has a **role-specific** member set, not global `GENERATOR_STATES` (compressor OFF/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; grid AVAILABLE/LOST; generator STOPPED/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; bus POWERED/UNPOWERED). Reject unmapped members. Foundation policy roles stay NUMBER with `covered_by='FOUNDATION_CONTROL'`; they are selected from typed properties and have no channel/binding row.
75-
76-**C — thirteen properties.** Add exactly these definitions (carrier/type, kind, canonical unit): `cold-thermal-capacity` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/degC); `cold-heat-leak` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kW/degC); `cold-cooling-capacity` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, kW); `cold-door-heat` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/min); `cold-control-low` and `cold-control-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-policy-low` and `cold-policy-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-min-excursion` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, min); `cold-return-hysteresis` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-start-timeout` (GENERATOR, CONTROL, min); `cold-probe-error` (TEMPERATURE_PROBE, PHYSICAL, degC); `cold-compressor-start-delay` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, min). The thirteenth property makes the stated 1-minute compressor delay a declared, frozen physical parameter. Extend property `UNIT_RANGES` with inclusive `kWh/degC:[0,10000]`, `kW/degC:[0,1000]`, `kWh/min:[0,1000]`, `degC:[-40,60]`, `kW:[0,100000]`, `min:[0,100000]`; these are parser-wide finite safety bounds, while cold-specific validation requires thermal capacity/leak/cooling capacity >0, door heat >=0, compressor delay >=0, start timeout >0, probe error >=0, min excursion >0, policy/control low < high, and `0 <= hysteresis < control_high-control_low`. Avoid making all Celsius values positive: signed temperatures are valid. Enforce carrier types in the cold Foundation validator after parsing; do not weaken old property checks. Add `degC` to `RATING_UNITS` only for an actual declared temperature rating, never as a substitute for these properties.
77-
78:**D–I — selected read and UI.** Until a persisted commissioning-evidence record exists, always return `commissioning:{state:'UNCONFIRMED',evidence_ids:[]}`. Gateway contact comes from the selected Site gateway-health read: `CURRENT -> RECENT`, `STALE -> STALE`, `UNKNOWN` with no diagnostic -> `NEVER`; `as_of` is the actual diagnostic receive time or null. If a diagnostic exists but its freshness is UNKNOWN, report `STALE` with limitation `Contact time cannot be verified`, never claim recent contact. This is contact, not equipment health. W2-01 publishes `bindings[].cadence_seconds=300` as the fixed `cold-roles-1` channel contract, not from stored binding rows or readings; W2-02's frozen `cold-publication/1` profile must declare the same 300-second sampling cadence and refuse a mismatch. Event channels are transition-driven and return `cadence_seconds=null`, with a `transition` publication mode. The gateway's `declared_cadence_seconds` is diagnostic heartbeat metadata and must not fill this field. For a selected COLDCHAIN Site, `/sites/:id/foundation`, `/sites/:id/foundation/readiness`, and `/sites/:id/foundation/sld` render `cold-foundation`, `cold-readiness`, `cold-sld`; MINIGRID keeps its existing views, and wrong-type cold API reads return 404. Add title-map entries `cold-readiness:2 = Gateway status · {selected gateway display name or 'No gateway selected'}` and `cold-sld:1 = {selected Site name} · {selected topology type}` when a type is declared, otherwise Site name alone. Values come from the selected read. No mock Site/gateway name is a fallback.
79-
80-**W2-01 contact null closure (`D-2026-10-17-f5-subtabs-and-contact`).** `contact.state` is never null in a successful cold Foundation/readiness response. If there is no gateway-health store or no matching selected gateway/diagnostic, return `NEVER` with `as_of:null`; do not manufacture a receive time. For a registered selected gateway with no diagnostic, the UI may say `No contact received yet`. If no gateway is selected or registered, say `No gateway selected`/`No gateway registered` and do not say that a particular gateway was never contacted. Preserve a typed limitation identifying the absent health read or gateway match. A health-read error is a read failure with an explicit unavailable limitation, not evidence for a recent or stale contact state. `NEVER` here means no contact is recorded in the available selected read; it proves neither equipment condition nor physical non-contact. Cover both missing-store and unmatched-gateway cases, including API enum, null `as_of`, and UI wording, in W2-01 review tests.
81-
82-**W2-02 channel closure.** Add `grid_power_event` / METER, EVENT `POWER_LOSS|POWER_RESTORED`, ONE, `event` unit. Replace the two impossible `backup_request` and `backup_result` channel bindings with **one** required `backup_start_event` / GENERATOR, EVENT `START_REQUEST|START_RESULT`, ONE; require both kinds in capability/readiness, while one registered `(generator-controller,start-event)` channel carries both. `grid_state` remains its separate `(grid-meter,state)` channel. `transfer_result`, `door_event` and `manual_temperature` keep separate channels. The W2-00 closed v4 table and W2-01 snapshot rows must name these exact roles; no channel may be bound twice. On cold panels replace status labels `Mapped`/`Not mapped` with `Configured`/`Needs a source`; use `Reporting`/`No report received` only when an actual selected evidence/contact read proves it. Keep panel titles fixed by the rendered-panel manifest.

141-## 6. W2-04 consequence, reads and UI
142-
143-`PRODUCT_EXPOSURE` is a bounded thermal exposure indicator in `degC_min`, **not product quantity, spoilage or loss**. `cold_stock_quantity`, `cold_stock_unit_value` (currency/unit) and a product-specific reviewed `cold_exposure_rule` would be needed for a future valued risk scenario; W2 creates no default and still forbids an amount without an approved rule. `RECORDED_REPAIR` amount requires exact accepted work/cost record, currency, time and non-overlap; no such source exists in W2, so it is `UNPRICED`. All consequences pin FindingRevision, episode revision, accepted IDs, assumption revision IDs, model/formula version, source gate and overlap group. Missing/contradictory probe or policy, stale projection, mixed/unknown provenance, undeclared stock or cost → `UNPRICED`/`WITHHELD` with null amount. Never sum backup and excursion exposure; no zero amount as stand-in for unknown. The existing LR current-money and S4-05 source gates still apply (`backend/migrations/versions/0010_financial_assumptions.py:1-30`; `backend/migrations/versions/0011_financial_consequences.py:43-69`; `.ai/DECISIONS.md:5-15`).
144-
145:All routes below use Site code, authenticated tenant scope, UTC `from,to` (end exclusive), optional UTC `known_at` (default current committed watermark), max 31-day window, and return `{site_id,window:{from,to,known_at},currentness:{state,watermark,latest_relevant_evidence_at,pending_since},source_provenance,limitations,...}`. `watermark` is latest included ingestion disposition `decided_at` plus assessment revision ID; a selected historical cutoff cannot claim today's currentness. Lists use opaque `(time,id)` cursor, `limit` default 50 max 200 and `{items,next_cursor}`. Shared refusal envelope `{error:{code,message}}`: 401 unauthenticated, 403 wrong tenant/source-gated disclosure, 404 Site/record not found or wrong type, 409 unresolved selected revision or recompute conflict, 422 malformed/window/range/unknown parameter, 503 ledger/projection unavailable; a valid missing claim returns 200 with `UNKNOWN`/null, never 404 or a fabricated zero. Existing evidence/Finding paths continue to serve exact links (`backend/assetops_backend/evidence_api.py:157-161`; `backend/assetops_backend/finding_api.py:102-189`).

208-Cold money work is triggered by appended cold FindingRevision or episode revision, relevant assumption publication or source-gate change; a migration trigger inserts append-only dirty `(site,window,domain='cold_chain')` work with unique dirty key and `DO NOTHING` duplicate suppression. `financials/recompute.py` runs the consequence worker; it selects unfinished work lacking a successful attempt, locks per work ID, selects the exact settled assessment/Finding basis, writes immutable consequence/evidence rows through `postgres_consequence_store.py`, and appends a `SUCCEEDED` attempt in the same transaction. Failure appends a `FAILED` attempt with an error code, leaving work retryable; no work status UPDATE exists. `tools/writer_guard_probes.py` gains pass probes for each owner/table, message-target conflict and attempt append, and fail probes for runtime work INSERT, foreign writer, immutable UPDATE/DELETE, `DO UPDATE`, and event partial-index conflict target. Include double-collision probes: message+event must choose message; event+session with a free message must choose event. `configuration-persistence.ps1` counts probes and fails closed when an owner is absent. `overlap_group` is SHA-256 of canonical `(tenant_id,site_id,window_start,window_end,room_asset_id)` prefixed `cold:`; excursion and backup consequences for that room/window share it.

Show the actual window, evidence `as_of`, committed disposition watermark and registered-simulation provenance; never call that replay live or move its timestamps. A future window cannot be `CURRENT` before its `to` boundary has elapsed. State precedence: overlapping queued/failed newer work -> `PENDING_RECOMPUTE`; explicit `known_at` older than the latest committed relevant disposition -> `HISTORICAL`; no settled selection or qualifying probe -> `UNKNOWN`; latest qualifying probe more than 10 minutes before `to` -> `STALE_SOURCE`; otherwise `CURRENT`.  `temperature` adds `policy:{low_deg_c,high_deg_c,return_hysteresis_deg_c,min_excursion_min,probe_asset_id,room_asset_id,as_of,limitation}`, `points:[{at,value,quality,evidence_id,as_of,limitation}]`, `coverage:{known_points,expected_points,ratio}`, `gaps:[{start,end,reason,expected_points,received_points,as_of,limitation}]`, `excursions:[{episode_id,revision_id,direction,start_bounds:{earliest,latest},end_bounds:{earliest,latest},duration_bounds:{low,high},degree_minute_bounds:{low,high},peak_observed,claim_level,as_of,limitation}]`, `excluded:[{evidence_id,reason,as_of,limitation}]`. `backup-chain` adds `chains:[{correlation_id,episode_id,revision_id,steps:[{kind,at,outcome,evidence_id,state,as_of,limitation}],missing_steps,claim_level,start_timeout_min,as_of,limitation}]`, `contradictions:[{correlation_id,evidence_ids,reason,as_of,limitation}]`; step order is GRID, START_REQUEST, START_RESULT, GENERATOR, TRANSFER when bound, BUS, COMPRESSOR, PROBE. Each evidence/Analysis link retains the selected window/cutoff and exact accepted/revision ID. Evaluate the 10-minute probe recency at the selected half-open window's `to` boundary, using the latest qualifying probe `source_time` in that window. Show the actual window, evidence `as_of`, committed disposition watermark and registered-simulation provenance; never call that replay live or move its timestamps. State precedence: overlapping queued/failed newer work -> `PENDING_RECOMPUTE`; explicit `known_at` older than the latest committed relevant disposition -> `HISTORICAL`; no settled selection, no qualifying probe, or a future `to` boundary -> `UNKNOWN`; latest qualifying probe more than 10 minutes before `to` -> `STALE_SOURCE`; otherwise `CURRENT`. An explicit historical cutoff stays historical even if probe recency passes. `known_at` remains the committed disposition cutoff and is not the freshness clock. Test later wall-clock replay viewing, an actually stale selected window, an older explicit cutoff and a pending newer job.
